# Verified versions

Every tool, flag, version and schema this project depends on is verified against its official source before the plan, the templates or the gates rely on it. Official sources are package registries (npm, Packagist, PyPI, Docker Hub), release tags resolved with `git ls-remote`, the vendor's documentation, and the tool's own source at the pinned tag. Each row records the value, where it was verified and the date. A scheduled workflow flags every entry whose **Verified on** date is older than 90 days, so it is re-checked before it goes stale.

## How to read this file

- **Verified value** is the final value. Where an independent second pass disagreed with the first pass, the value shown is the corrected one.
- **Notes** start with the verification status:
  - *Re-checked*: confirmed by an independent second pass against primary sources.
  - *Re-checked, refined*: confirmed, with a nuance or correction added by the second pass.
  - *Corrected on re-check*: the first pass was wrong; the value shown is the second pass's.
  - *Corrected at write time* / *write-time check*: resolved again from the primary source on 2026-09-25 while this file was written.
  - *Found on re-check*: a fact that only the second pass surfaced. It was verified once, by that pass.
  - *Single pass*: verified once against the listed source and not re-checked. These are non-critical, high-confidence items.
- Values that could not be confirmed are **not** in the domain tables. They are listed under [Unverified — re-check before use](#unverified--re-check-before-use), together with pending upstream events.
- "Sandbox" or "local reproduction" in the Source column means the behaviour was reproduced by installing the pinned version in a throwaway project.
- Pins are exact on purpose: a full commit SHA for GitHub Actions, and an exact version plus checksum for binaries and MCP servers.
- The Claude Code plugins and marketplaces table was re-checked by two independent passes. Where they disagreed, the item is listed under [Unverified — re-check before use](#unverified--re-check-before-use).

## Agent Skills and Gemini CLI

The portable package is `skills/cross-stack-standard/`. Its `SKILL.md` uses `name` and `description`
frontmatter and includes the generated track rule pages. Gemini CLI 0.61.0 installed a copy of this
folder into a temporary workspace with `gemini skills install <local-path> --scope workspace`; the
CLI printed the expected skill name and description. A remote GitHub install has not been exercised.

| Tool / item | Verified value | Source | Verified on | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gemini CLI Agent Skills | `gemini skills install <source> --scope user\|workspace --path <repo-subdir>`; `SKILL.md` requires `name` and `description` | <https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/skills/><br><https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/creating-skills/> | 2026-10-10 | *Local smoke install passed* with Gemini CLI 0.61.0; public GitHub install awaits repository publication. |

## Claude Code: hooks, skills, settings and plugin loading

Scope: Hook events, matchers, exit codes and decisions; skill and CLAUDE.md limits; plugin and marketplace settings keys; permission rule matching; plugin CLI. 28 entries.

| Tool / item | Verified value | Source | Verified on | Notes |

| Hook event: SessionStart matcher values | startup\|resume\|clear\|compact\|fork | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* Since v2.1.214, forked sessions (`--fork-session`, `/fork`, `/branch`) report source `fork`, not `resume`, so a matcher without `fork` skips them. Use `startup\|resume\|clear\|compact\|fork` or omit the matcher. |
| Hook exit code 2 behavior | Exit 2 blocks only on events that can block: PreToolUse, UserPromptSubmit, UserPromptExpansion, Stop, SubagentStop, TeammateIdle, TaskCreated, TaskCompleted, ConfigChange, PostToolBatch, PreCompact, PreModelSwitch, Elicitation, ElicitationResult and the Worktree events. PostToolUse CANNOT block: 'Shows stderr to Claude; the tool already ran'. The other two parts are correct: 'even a JSON permissionDecision of "allow" can't override it', and the message is the JSON blocking reason, otherwise stderr. Exit 1 does not block. A hook that can't start, or a timed-out PreToolUse command hook, fails open. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Corrected on re-check.* |
| PostToolUse/PreToolUse tool matchers | PreToolUse matches every tool name except EndConversation, for example Bash, PowerShell, Edit, Write, Read, Glob, Grep, Agent, Workflow, WebFetch, WebSearch, AskUserQuestion, ExitPlanMode and mcp\_\_&lt;server&gt;\_\_&lt;tool&gt;. A plugin's own MCP tools are named mcp\_\_plugin\_&lt;plugin&gt;\_&lt;server&gt;\_\_&lt;tool&gt;. The notebook tool is `NotebookEdit`; no tool is named 'Notebook', and `^Notebook` is only a regex example. MultiEdit is described as 'the legacy MultiEdit tool'. The current file-editing tools are Edit, Write and NotebookEdit. A matcher made only of letters, digits, \_, -, spaces, \| and , is an exact list; any other character makes it an unanchored JS regex. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/tools-reference.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Corrected on re-check.* Current file-editing tools: `Edit`, `Write`, `NotebookEdit`. `MultiEdit` is legacy, so a matcher entry for it never fires. Normalise backslashes in `tool_input.file_path` on Windows. |

Only `plugin.json` belongs in `.claude-plugin/`; `hooks/` goes at the plugin root. |
| CLAUDE.md size guidance | Target under 200 lines per file. Files over 200 lines consume more context and reduce adherence. Soft cap at 4 MiB. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/memory.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Single pass.* |

Plugins cannot ship rules (see the "What plugins cannot ship" row). |
| extraKnownMarketplaces JSON schema | {"marketplaceName": {"source": {"source": "github", "repo": "owner/repo", "ref": "..."}}} - optional 'ref' field for pinning | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* Re-check: Correct shape. As a MARKETPLACE source, `github` takes repo, ref (branch or tag), path and sparsePaths. It has NO `sha`; `sha` exists only on PLUGIN sources inside marketplace.json. An optional `autoUpdate` boolean sits next to `source`. Entries in a repo's .claude/settings.json are honoured only after the workspace-trust dialog. In an untrusted folder, including -p runs, they are ignored with no message. `additionalMarketplaces` is an alias from v2.1.232. |
| enabledPlugins setting format | Object mapping `plugin-name@marketplace-name` to a boolean, e.g. `{"cross-stack-standard@cross-stack-standard": true}`. Keys must include the `@marketplace` part; a bare plugin id is not the documented form. Project settings override user settings; opt out locally with `false` in `.claude/settings.local.json`; a managed `false` blocks the plugin at every scope. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* |
| Managed settings: strictKnownMarketplaces, allowManagedHooksOnly | Managed-only settings. strictKnownMarketplaces allowlists marketplace sources; allowManagedHooksOnly restricts hooks to deployed ones. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* Under `allowManagedHooksOnly`, plugin hooks run only when managed `enabledPlugins` force-enables the plugin; project and user hooks are blocked. |
| Windows hook command execution (shell type) | The docs specify it explicitly. Shell form (no `args`) runs `sh -c` on macOS/Linux, Git Bash on Windows, and PowerShell when Git Bash isn't installed. The `shell` field accepts "bash" or "powershell" (default bash, or powershell on Windows without Git Bash) and is ignored when `args` is set. Exec form (`args` present) spawns directly with no shell on any platform. On Windows, exec form needs a real executable: .cmd/.bat shims can't be spawned, so use "command": "node" with the script path in args. The docs say to 'Prefer exec form for any hook that references a path placeholder.' | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Corrected on re-check.* |
| PreToolUse deny decision in bypassPermissions mode | PreToolUse deny blocks the tool 'even in bypassPermissions mode' | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* Re-check: True, but the quote is on the hooks GUIDE page, not the hooks reference. It also holds with --dangerously-skip-permissions, and PreToolUse fires in every mode including dontAsk. Exit 2 routes the same way as deny. The deny reason is shown to Claude. |
| PreToolUse `permissionDecision` values | `allow`, `deny`, `ask`, `defer`. Precedence across hooks: deny &gt; defer &gt; ask &gt; allow. `approve` and `block` are deprecated top-level `decision` values that map to allow and deny. For `ask`, the permissionDecisionReason is shown to the user but not to Claude. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permission-modes.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* Only `deny` (or exit 2) is documented to block in bypassPermissions mode; `ask` shows the normal prompt and is not among the actions no mode auto-approves. A permission `ask` rule (e.g. `Edit(phpstan.neon.dist)`) prompts in every mode. |
| Agent Skills `name` and `description` limits | `name`: 1–64 characters, lowercase letters, digits and hyphens, no leading/trailing/consecutive hyphens, must not contain the reserved words `anthropic` or `claude`. `description`: 1–1024 characters. Claude Code truncates description + when\_to\_use at 1,536 characters in the skill listing. | <https://agentskills.io/specification><br><https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/agents-and-tools/agent-skills/best-practices><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* `cross-stack-standard` (20 characters) complies. |
| Stop hook loop guard and continuation cap | Stop hook input carries `stop_hook_active`. Claude Code overrides the hook and ends the turn after 8 consecutive blocks. Stop hooks fire whenever Claude finishes responding, not only at task completion. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks-guide.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* A Stop gate should skip work when nothing changed since the last passing run. |
| Hook timeouts | Default 600 s for command hooks; lowered to 30 s for command, http and mcp\_tool hooks on UserPromptSubmit, PreModelSwitch and PostModelSwitch. A timed-out command hook's output is discarded and does not block the tool call. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* Enforce a shorter budget (e.g. 90 s) inside the script and set the hook `timeout` above it. |
| Hook output visibility and fail-open | Stderr from a hook that exits 0 goes to the debug log only; Claude never sees it. User-visible notices use the JSON `systemMessage` field on stdout. A hook that cannot start (e.g. `node` missing) is a non-blocking error: the action proceeds with a hook-error notice. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* |
| SessionStart context output | Plain stdout from SessionStart is added as context, capped at 10,000 characters. The docs advise factual statements rather than imperative system instructions, because text framed as out-of-band commands can trigger prompt-injection defenses. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* |
| PowerShell tool and Windows paths | Claude Code has a native `PowerShell` tool (on by default on Windows without Git Bash). PreToolUse sees `tool_name` `PowerShell` with `tool_input.command`. On Windows, `tool_input.file_path` arrives with backslash separators. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/tools-reference.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* Command inspection needs the matcher `Bash\|PowerShell`. |
| Built-in protected paths | `.claude/` (except `.claude/worktrees`), `.mcp.json` and `lefthook.yml` are never auto-approved except in bypassPermissions; auto mode sends them to the classifier; dontAsk denies; `permissions.allow` rules do not pre-approve them. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permission-modes.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* Writes by an adopt/upgrade skill to these paths prompt the user. |
| Bash permission rule matching | `*` matches any text, including spaces, at any position; a trailing ` *` that is the rule's only wildcard also matches the bare command; `:*` is the same as a trailing ` *` and is recognised only at the end of a pattern. Rules are evaluated deny, then ask, then allow, across every settings scope. Compound commands are split on `&&`, `\|\|`, `;`, `\|`, `\|&`, `&` and newlines, and a deny or ask rule applies when any subcommand matches (subshells and command substitutions included). The wrappers `timeout`, `time`, `nice`, `nohup`, `stdbuf`, `command`, `builtin`, `noglob` and bare `xargs` are stripped; a deny or ask rule matches past any leading variable assignment. Rules match command text: `Bash(git push *)` does not stop `git -C . push`, `git -c … push` or `git 'push'`, nor a program run by path or inside `sh -c`. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions.md#wildcard-patterns><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions.md#compound-commands><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions.md#bash-rule-limits> | 2026-09-26 | *Single pass.* The repository's `.claude/settings.json` relies on it; `test/scripts/ci/claude-settings.test.mjs` encodes these semantics and the commands each rule must match. Claude Code warns at startup only for an allow rule with a `*` before the subcommand. |
| `claude plugin` CLI | `details <name>` (component inventory and projected token cost; the plugin must be loaded), `validate <path>` (`--json`; `--strict` turns warnings into exit 1), `tag` (creates `{name}--v{version}` git tags and checks that plugin.json and the marketplace entry agree), `eval`, `install`, `uninstall`, `enable`, `disable`, `update`, `list`, `prune`, `init`, `marketplace`. `claude --plugin-dir <path>` loads a plugin for one session. | claude 2.1.296 `--version` and `plugin validate --strict` (local)<br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/cli-reference.md> | 2026-10-12 | *Re-checked for release.* npm `latest` and `next` are 2.1.296; `stable` is 2.1.287. Both marketplace and plugin validation passed. |
| Always-on cost reported by `claude plugin details` | Counts skill names and descriptions. Hooks show as "harness-only — no model context cost", although SessionStart stdout is injected into every session. Skills with `disable-model-invocation: true` put no description in runtime context, but `details` still counts them. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/measure.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/cli-reference.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills.md><br>local reproduction (claude 2.1.296) | 2026-10-12 | *Measured for release.* `details` reports ~927 tokens and counts the five user-only skills; runtime `/context` still needs an authenticated session. |
| Plugin `version` in plugin.json vs the marketplace entry | plugin.json `version` wins over the marketplace plugin entry's `version`. Equal values pass `claude plugin validate` with no warning; a mismatch warns ("plugin.json wins … the entry version is silently ignored"), which fails `--strict`. The docs advise setting it in one place only. A pinned `version` keeps users on the cached copy until the string changes. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/marketplace-reference.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace.md#release-a-new-version><br>local reproduction (claude 2.1.280) | 2026-09-25 | *Corrected on re-check.* The earlier reading, that validate warns whenever both are set, followed the marketplace reference's wording; reproduction shows that only a mismatch warns. Stamp plugin.json and the marketplace `metadata.version`; leave the plugin entry `version` unset. |
| Repository-level plugin enablement | `extraKnownMarketplaces` in a repo's `.claude/settings.json` applies only after the workspace-trust dialog; in untrusted folders, including `-p` runs, entries are ignored silently. A plugin listed by relative path loads from the marketplace copy once the entry applies; an external-source plugin shows "enabled in project settings but isn't installed" until each user installs it. In CI set `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGIN_INSTALL=1`. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* `additionalMarketplaces` is an alias from v2.1.232. |
| What plugins cannot ship | No rules component; a CLAUDE.md at the plugin root is not loaded (`claude plugin validate` warns). Plugin settings honour only `agent` and `subagentStatusLine`, so plugins cannot ship permission rules. Plugin skills support `paths:` frontmatter for file-scoped activation; brace globs such as `src/**/*.{ts,tsx}` work. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/memory.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/components.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* Rules ship as templates that an adopt step copies to `.claude/rules/`. |

## Claude Code plugins and marketplaces

Scope: plugin layout and `plugin.json`; `marketplace.json`, plugin and marketplace sources, reserved names; versions, dependencies, updates, pinning and release channels; the plugin CLI (`validate`, `details`, `install`, `update`, `--plugin-dir`, `eval`); skills and hooks inside a plugin; team rollout, trust and managed settings; installing the CLI in CI. 65 entries.

Local reproductions used Claude Code 2.1.280 with a throwaway `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`; npm `latest` is 2.1.282, and rows name the 2.1.281 and 2.1.282 changes that affect them. Every critical row was re-checked by two independent passes: *Re-checked* here means both confirmed it. Where the two passes disagreed, the item is under [Unverified — re-check before use](#unverified--re-check-before-use).

| Tool / item | Verified value | Source | Verified on | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Plugin directory layout | The manifest is `<plugin-root>/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`. Every other component (`skills/`, `commands/`, `agents/`, `hooks/`, `.mcp.json`, …) sits at the plugin root: "Only `plugin.json` goes inside `.claude-plugin/`. Components saved there don't load." | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/create.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md#manifest-file><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/troubleshooting.md#plugin-loads-but-its-skills-are-missing> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* The older pages `/docs/en/plugins-reference`, `/plugin-marketplaces` and `/discover-plugins` now serve the same content as `plugins/manifest-reference`, `plugins/create-marketplace` and `plugins/install`. |
| Components misplaced inside `.claude-plugin/` | Not detected. A plugin with `.claude-plugin/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` or `.claude-plugin/hooks/hooks.json` passes `claude plugin validate` with no warning about them and loads without an error. The only signal is `Skills (0)` / `Hooks (0)` in `claude plugin details`. | local reproduction (claude 2.1.280): `claude plugin validate`, `claude --plugin-dir <dir> plugin details`, `claude --plugin-dir <dir> plugin list --json` | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* Add a repository check that fails when anything other than `plugin.json` (or `marketplace.json` at the marketplace root) is under a `.claude-plugin/` directory. |
| Standard plugin layout (default locations) | `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` (optional); `skills/<name>/SKILL.md`; `commands/*.md` (legacy form); `agents/*.md` (subfolders become part of the agent name); `hooks/hooks.json` (top-level `hooks` key, same shape as settings hooks); `.mcp.json`; `.lsp.json`; `output-styles/`; `workflows/*.js`; `themes/`; `monitors/monitors.json`; `bin/` (on the Bash tool's PATH while the plugin is enabled; claude.ai and Cowork don't install a plugin that has it); `settings.json` (only `agent` and `subagentStatusLine` apply). A root `SKILL.md` with no `skills/` directory and no `skills` key loads as a single skill. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md#standard-layout> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* A `hooks.json` without a top-level `hooks` key fails validate (`must have hooks (the hook matchers) or modules (hooks modules), or both`) and loads no hooks. validate accepts the undocumented `modules` key. |
| `plugin.json` required fields | Only `name` is required. The manifest itself is optional: without it the name comes from the marketplace entry, or from the directory name under `--plugin-dir`. validate warns, and does not error, on a missing `version`, `description` or `author`; `--strict` turns those warnings into exit 1. An empty object fails with `name: Invalid input`. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md#fields><br>local reproduction (claude 2.1.280): `claude plugin validate` on `{"name":"cross-stack-standard"}` with and without `--strict` | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* |
| `plugin.json` fields and types | `$schema` (ignored at load); `name` (required, kebab-case); `displayName`; `version` (string, not semver-checked; holds users on a version until it changes); `description`; `author` (object: `name` required, `email` and `url` optional); `homepage` (must parse as a URL or the plugin fails to load); `repository` (not validated); `license` (SPDX string, not checked); `keywords` (string[]); `metadata` (free-form object, v2.1.222+); `defaultEnabled` (boolean, default true; the marketplace entry's value overrides it); `dependencies`; `settings` (only `agent` and `subagentStatusLine` apply); `userConfig`; `channels`; `skills` (path or paths; adds to `skills/`); `commands` (path, paths or object map; replaces `commands/`); `agents` (replaces `agents/`); `hooks` (path, object or array; merges with `hooks/hooks.json`); `mcpServers` (path, `.mcpb`/`.dxt` bundle, URL, object or array; merges with `.mcp.json`); `lspServers`; `outputStyles` (replaces `output-styles/`); `workflows` (replaces `workflows/`); `experimental` (`themes`, `monitors`, `evals`). | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md#fields><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md#how-each-key-combines-with-its-default-location> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* Unknown top-level keys are stripped with a validate warning (`Unknown field '…'. Claude Code ignores it at load time.`); unknown keys inside `userConfig`, `channels`, `lspServers` or `monitors` entries are errors. A root `settings.json` key takes precedence over the same key under `settings`. A full manifest (author, homepage, repository, license, keywords, `defaultEnabled`, `$schema`) passed `validate --strict --json`. From 2.1.281 validate no longer reports listing keys such as `privacyPolicyUrl` and `supportUrl` as unknown. |
| Plugin component path rules | Every component path is relative to the plugin root, starts with `./`, must exist and must resolve inside the plugin root. A path containing `..` fails validate (`Path contains ".." which could be a path traversal attempt`); absolute paths and paths without `./` are invalid. `skills` also accepts `"."` (v2.1.221+). On macOS and Linux a path containing a backslash passes validate but is refused at load (`Path escapes plugin directory … its path contains a backslash`). validate does not check `outputStyles`, `lspServers`, `monitors` or `themes` paths, so a missing one fails only at load. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md#path-rules><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#paths-that-escape-the-plugin-directory><br>local reproduction (claude 2.1.280) | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* See the `plugin list --json` row for the load-time check that catches these. |
| Installed plugin copies and files outside the plugin | A marketplace plugin, including a relative-path plugin from a git-hosted marketplace, is copied to `~/.claude/plugins/cache/<marketplace>/<plugin>/<version>/`. "Files outside the plugin directory aren't copied", so a script that reads a path above the plugin root (such as `../shared`) finds nothing. Symlinks at copy time: a target inside the plugin is kept as a relative symlink; a target elsewhere in the same marketplace is dereferenced and its content copied into the cache; a target outside the marketplace is skipped. Plugins installed from a local path or a copy-mode command source keep only symlinks inside the plugin. `--plugin-dir` plugins and relative-path plugins from a local-directory marketplace load in place. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#in-place-and-copied-plugins><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace.md#share-files-within-a-marketplace-with-symlinks> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* The first pass read the symlink rule too narrowly; the loading page's wording about links between plugins applies to declared component paths. In-place loading hides missing files during development, so test a copy of the plugin directory on its own. A symlink at the plugin root passed `validate --strict`; a symlink inside `skills/` gave the warning `1 entry here is a symlink and was not read`, which fails `--strict`. |
| Node.js dependencies of a copied plugin | When copying a plugin to the cache, Claude Code installs its npm dependencies only if the plugin root has `package.json` plus a supported lockfile: `bun.lock`/`bun.lockb` → `bun install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts`; `npm-shrinkwrap.json`/`package-lock.json` → `npm ci --ignore-scripts`. The install has a 60 s timeout and can't be turned off. `yarn.lock` and `pnpm-lock.yaml` are skipped; "A plugin with a package.json and no lockfile is skipped without a log entry"; a failure never blocks the plugin. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#node-js-package-dependencies> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* A workspace member has no lockfile of its own, and a plugin loaded in place resolves modules from the repository's `node_modules`, which hides missing dependencies. Hook scripts that use only Node built-ins avoid this path. |
| Plugin name rules | Docs: non-empty; no spaces, `@`, `:`, path separators, control or bidirectional-formatting characters; use kebab-case. Every component is namespaced under the plugin name (agent `reviewer` in plugin `deploy-tools` appears as `deploy-tools:reviewer`). validate on 2.1.280: a space is an error (`Plugin name cannot contain spaces`); `CrossStack`, `cross_stack`, `a@b`, `a:b`, `a/b` and `x.y` get only a warning (`is not kebab-case. Claude Code accepts it, but the Claude.ai marketplace sync requires kebab-case`); `cross-stack-standard` passes with no warning. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/manifest-reference.md#name><br>local reproduction (claude 2.1.280) | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* validate is looser than the documented rule for `@`, `:` and `/`. |
| Plugin version resolution and update detection | Computed version = plugin.json `version`, else the marketplace entry's `version`, else derived from the source: a relative path inside a git-hosted marketplace gives the commit SHA of the installed directory; github, url and git-subdir give a 12-character commit SHA; archive a 12-character sha256; npm and non-git local sources `unknown`. The version also names the cache directory. `claude plugin update` and auto-update install only when the computed version differs, so a pinned `version` keeps users on the cached copy "however many commits they push". To track commits instead, omit `version` from both plugin.json and the entry. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#how-claude-code-computes-the-version><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace.md#release-a-new-version> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* Bump plugin.json `version` on every release, or users receive nothing. |
| `version` in both plugin.json and the marketplace entry | Equal values pass validate with no warning, also with `--strict`. Different values give a warning (`Entry declares version "0.2.0" but …/plugin.json says "0.1.0". At install time, plugin.json wins … the entry version is silently ignored.`): exit 0, or exit 1 with `--strict`. `claude plugin tag` refuses a mismatch (`Version mismatch`) and passes when they agree. The docs advise: "Don't set version in both plugin.json and the marketplace entry." A marketplace top-level `version` or `metadata.version` gives no warning. | local reproduction (claude 2.1.280): `claude plugin validate` with the entry version absent, equal and different; `claude plugin tag --dry-run`<br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace.md#release-a-new-version><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/cli-reference.md#plugin-tag> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* The marketplace reference's entry-field row ("plugin.json takes precedence and `claude plugin validate` warns") reads as if any double-set warns; only a mismatch does. | `plugin.json` `dependencies` | Entries are `"name"` (same marketplace), `"name@marketplace"`, or an object with `name` (required), `marketplace` and `version`, a semver range such as `~2.1.0`, `^2.0`, `>=1.4` or `=2.1.0`. A range skips pre-releases unless written like `^2.0.0-0`. There is no optional or soft dependency: they are "Plugins that must be enabled for this one to work". `claude plugin disable` refuses to disable a plugin that an enabled plugin depends on; if a dependency is missing or disabled anyway, the dependent is disabled at load (`Dependency "x@m" is not installed`). | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/dependencies.md#declare-a-dependency-with-a-version-constraint><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/cli-reference.md#plugin-disable><br>local reproduction (claude 2.1.280) | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* `validate --strict` silently accepts unknown keys in a dependency object (e.g. `"optional": true`) and an invalid range. The key is ignored, and the plugin stays disabled for anyone without the dependency. |
| Dependency version constraints and tags | A range resolves against git tags named `<plugin-name>--v<version>` in the repository that hosts the dependency: its own repository for github, url and git-subdir sources, the marketplace repository for relative-path sources. With no satisfying tag, a plugin with its own repository fails to install (`has no git tag satisfying`); a relative-path plugin installs the marketplace's current copy and the dependent stays disabled (`Requires "x@m" ~2.1.0, installed 3.0.0`). npm, archive and command sources are checked against the dependency's plugin.json `version`. `claude plugin tag` creates the tags. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/dependencies.md#how-a-constraint-resolves-against-tags> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* |
| Cross-marketplace dependencies | Allowed only if the root marketplace's `marketplace.json` lists the other marketplace in `allowCrossMarketplaceDependenciesOn`, or the user already has the dependency installed and enabled at the same scope. | `claude plugin details` | Takes no flags. The plugin must be loaded: installed, in a skills directory, or passed with `--plugin-dir`/`--plugin-url` in the same command, e.g. `claude --plugin-dir ./plugins/cross-stack-standard plugin details cross-stack-standard`; otherwise it prints `Plugin "x" not found…` and exits 1. Output: a header (display name, name, version, description, source), the component inventory, and "Projected token cost" with `Always-on: ~N tok added to every session` plus per-component always-on and on-invoke figures. The always-on figure counts each skill, agent and command name plus `description` and `when_to_use`. | Release channels | "Claude Code has no release-channel concept, and one marketplace serves one version of each plugin at a time." The documented recipe hosts two marketplaces with different `name` values whose entries point at different refs (e.g. `stable` and `latest`): "Give the two refs different plugin.json versions, or omit version so the commit SHA distinguishes them. Updates are detected by comparing versions, so a ref that moves without a version change leaves users on the cached copy." Admins assign channels per group through separate endpoint-managed settings or gateway policies; server-managed settings deliver one configuration per organisation. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace.md#run-release-channels><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org.md#assign-release-channels-to-user-groups> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* A branch pin (e.g. `#dev`) whose plugin.json keeps the last released version delivers nothing after the first install. |

The fix is to edit or remove the settings entry. When a declared source changes in settings, Claude Code re-fetches it and shows `Plugins changed. Run /reload-plugins to activate.` The `/plugin install --marketplace` flow reports `Marketplace "<name>" is already added from a different source`. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/troubleshooting.md#cannot-add-marketplace-its-network-source-differs><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#plugins-and-marketplaces-that-arent-on-disk-at-session-start> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* `marketplace add` writes its own entry to user settings, so a pin is moved by editing that `ref` (or by removing the marketplace, which uninstalls its plugins, and adding it again). A SHA pin exists only on plugin entries with github, url or git-subdir sources (`ref` plus `sha`), not on marketplace sources and not on relative-path entries. The community catalog pins nearly every entry to a commit SHA and refuses to install other commits. The docs' "Hold users on one version" options: an entry `ref` plus `sha`, `#ref` on add, or `<plugin>--v<version>` tags for dependency ranges. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace.md#hold-users-on-one-version><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/marketplace-reference.md#plugin-sources><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/security.md#marketplace-tiers> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* Users of a marketplace whose plugin entry is a relative path can pin by tag or branch only, so those tags must be immutable (see the immutable releases row). In hooks, `${...}` resolves anywhere in `command` and `args`, and the process receives `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`, `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA`, `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` and `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_OPTION_<KEY>`. The variables are not in the environment of Bash tool commands; in skill, command and agent content, write the `${...}` reference in the Markdown body and Claude Code substitutes the path when it loads the content. On Windows exec form needs a real executable, so use `"command": "node"` with the script path in `args`. Added in v2.1.139. `{"type": "command", "command": "node", "args": ["${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/session-start.mjs"], "timeout": 10}` under a SessionStart matcher `startup\|resume\|clear\|compact\|fork` passes `validate --strict`. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md#exec-form-and-shell-form><br><https://raw.githubusercontent.com/anthropics/claude-code/main/CHANGELOG.md> (2.1.139)<br>local reproduction (claude 2.1.280) | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* From 2.1.281 validate warns when a shell-form hook leaves `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` unquoted. |

| `claude plugin eval` | v2.1.269+. Runs a plugin's eval cases in isolated non-interactive sessions with only the plugin loaded, plus a no-plugin baseline arm by default (`--ablation with-without\|none`). A case is `<evaldir>/<case>/prompt.md` with frontmatter (`name`, `tags`, `plugins`, `runs` default 3, `max_turns` 10, `timeout_seconds` 300, `allowed_tools`, `model`, `env` with `EVAL_*`) plus `graders/*.md`, or a `case.yaml` (`schema_version` "1.1", `name`, execution and graders). Grader types: regex (on `last_message`, `trace`, `files`, `file` or `mock_calls`), `tool_used`, `tool_order`, `file_exists`, `llm`, `baseline`. The eval directory is `evals/` under the plugin, `experimental.evals`, or `--eval-dir` (relative plain directory names; absolute or `..` paths are refused). A single `prompt.md` or `case.yaml` can be the target; its `plugins` field lists plugin directories relative to the case directory. Runs need model credentials, and every run and judge call is billed. Default tools are read-only; Write, Edit and Bash need `--allow-tools`. CI flags: `--trust-plugin`, `--json results.json`, `--threshold`, `--model`, `--judge-model`, `--no-publish`, `--max-cost-usd`. When several files define the same marketplace name, the highest-precedence entry wins whole, with no field merge (v2.1.228+). `claude plugin marketplace add o6b7/cross-stack-standard --scope project` writes the entry. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference.md#extraknownmarketplaces><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference.md#enabledplugins><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace.md#register-the-marketplace-for-everyone-in-a-repository> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* Keep the canonical key names for teams on mixed client versions. |
| Trust and repository-declared plugins | A repository's `extraKnownMarketplaces` apply only after the workspace trust dialog is accepted. Untrusted folders ignore them without a message; when only a parent folder is trusted, no dialog is offered for them, while a fresh interactive session in the repository shows the normal trust dialog. In `-p` runs they apply only if the folder was trusted interactively or `hasTrustDialogAccepted` is set. Once they apply, a relative-path plugin loads from the marketplace copy with no per-user install; an external-source plugin shows `Plugin "<name>" is enabled in project settings but isn't installed` until each user runs `claude plugin install <name>@<marketplace> --scope project`. Cloud sessions never add repository-declared marketplaces. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org.md#require-plugins-per-repository><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#plugins-shared-through-a-repository><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions.md#what-runs-before-you-trust-a-folder> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* |
| `enabledPlugins` precedence and opt-out | Lowest to highest: `--add-dir`, user, project, local, the `--settings` flag, managed. A project `true` beats a user `false`; a user opts out in one repository with `false` in `.claude/settings.local.json`. Managed `true` force-enables; managed `false` blocks at every scope and hides the plugin. A plugin with no entry follows `defaultEnabled`. Uninstalling a project-enabled plugin prompts "Disable for me" (y, writes `false` to `settings.local.json`) or "Uninstall for everyone" (u). | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#find-where-a-plugin-is-enabled><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/install.md#uninstall-a-plugin-the-project-enables> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* |
| Managed settings for plugins | `strictKnownMarketplaces` (alias `allowedMarketplaces`): an allowlist of source objects matched exactly on repo or url plus ref and path, with `owner/*` wildcards, `hostPattern`/`pathPattern` regexes and `{"source": "skills-dir"}`; `[]` blocks everything. `blockedMarketplaces`. `enabledPlugins` `true`/`false` locks. `allowManagedHooksOnly: true` blocks user, project, local and plugin hooks except those of plugins that managed `enabledPlugins` force-enables, matched on the full `plugin@marketplace` id. Also `disableSideloadFlags`, `disableCommandPluginSources`, `strictPluginOnlyCustomization`, `pluginTrustMessage`, `syncClaudeAiPlugins`, `pluginSuggestionMarketplaces`. Server-managed settings have no per-group targeting. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org.md#control-matrix><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference.md#allowmanagedhooksonly> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* Under `allowManagedHooksOnly`, this plugin's hooks run only if managed `enabledPlugins` force-enables `cross-stack-standard@cross-stack-standard`. |
| Repository-shared plugin in `.claude/skills/` | `<project>/.claude/skills/<name>/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` loads for everyone in the repository as `<name>@skills-dir` after workspace trust, with no marketplace. It loads only from the primary working directory's `.claude/skills` (no parent-directory search); MCP servers need per-server approval and monitors don't load. Claude Code doesn't scan `.claude/plugins/`. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#plugins-shared-through-a-repository><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/create.md#load-a-plugin-for-everyone-in-one-repository> | 2026-09-25 | *Single pass.* |
| Claude Code CLI from npm (CI) | `@anthropic-ai/claude-code` latest/next 2.1.296 and stable 2.1.287 on npm; CI pins 2.1.296. `engines.node` is `>=22.0.0`; bin `claude`. A postinstall (`node install.cjs`) links a per-platform optional dependency (darwin arm64/x64, linux x64/arm64 plus musl, win32 x64/arm64), so optional dependencies must be allowed. npm 12 (npm's `latest`, 12.1.0) blocks that script for `npm install -g` and for `npx`/`npm exec`: the install exits 0 but leaves a placeholder, and `claude` prints `Error: claude native binary not installed.` Install with `npm install -g --allow-scripts=@anthropic-ai/claude-code @anthropic-ai/claude-code@2.1.296` (the same flag works for `npm exec`), or use the native installer, and check that `claude --version` prints the pinned version. | `npm view @anthropic-ai/claude-code version dist-tags engines bin scripts.postinstall optionalDependencies --json` (2026-10-12)<br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/setup.md#install-with-npm><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/troubleshoot-install.md#native-binary-not-found-after-npm-install><br>local reproduction with npm 12.1.0 (2026-09-25) | 2026-10-12 | npm 12 install behavior was reproduced on 2026-09-25; registry tags and the CI pin were rechecked on 2026-10-12. |
| Claude Code CLI native installer | macOS, Linux and WSL: `curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh \| bash` (pin with `\| bash -s 2.1.282`, or `-s stable`). Windows: `irm https://claude.ai/install.ps1 \| iex` or `install.cmd`. Homebrew: `brew install --cask claude-code`. WinGet: `winget install Anthropic.ClaudeCode`. Each release publishes a GPG-signed `manifest.json` of binary SHA-256 sums (key at <https://downloads.claude.ai/keys/claude-code.asc>). | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/setup.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Single pass.* |
| Plugin security model | "A Claude Code plugin you install can execute arbitrary code on your machine with your user privileges." Hooks and MCP servers run outside the sandbox and permission rules; skills and agents enter context as instructions; auto-update can change files that were reviewed. There is no plugin code signing; the only integrity checks are an archive source's `sha256` pin and the community catalog's commit-SHA pins. Official and community names are accepted only from github.com/anthropics/. The fixed install warning begins "Make sure you trust a plugin before installing, updating, or using it", and admins can append `pluginTrustMessage`. Review a plugin with `claude --plugin-dir <dir> plugin details <name>`. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/security.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Single pass.* |
| Plugin uninstall | `claude plugin uninstall <plugin> [-s scope] [--keep-data] [--prune -y] [--json]` removes the install and the `enabledPlugins` entry at that scope. From the last scope it deletes `~/.claude/plugins/data/<id>/` unless `--keep-data`. Cached version directories get an `.orphaned_at` marker and are swept 14 days later, and only while at least one install remains recorded; delete `~/.claude/plugins/cache/<marketplace>/<plugin>/` by hand to remove them at once. Auto-installed dependencies stay until `prune`. Files a plugin wrote into projects are not touched (the docs don't cover them). 2.1.282 fixed uninstall deleting a plugin's saved options when its settings file still enabled it or could not be read, and deleting saved options and secrets when the install list could not be read. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/cli-reference.md#plugin-uninstall><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/security.md#remove-a-plugin-you-no-longer-trust><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/loading.md#cleanup-of-previous-versions><br><https://raw.githubusercontent.com/anthropics/claude-code/main/CHANGELOG.md> (2.1.281, 2.1.282) | 2026-09-25 | *Corrected on re-check.* The first pass dated the saved-options fix to 2.1.281; one re-check pass agreed and one placed it in 2.1.282. Write-time check: the CHANGELOG lists both saved-options fixes under 2.1.282; 2.1.281 has other uninstall fixes. Older versions' files leave the cache 14 days after an update, so an upgrade step can't rely on them. |
| Always-on context a plugin adds | Every session with the plugin enabled carries the names and descriptions of its model-invocable skills, agents and commands, plus any SessionStart stdout or `additionalContext` (up to 10,000 characters per string). Plugin MCP tool schemas count under "MCP tools" in `/context`. Other hook events cost nothing unless they emit context. No per-plugin budget is documented; the official-marketplace UI highlights "Every turn" at 2,000 tokens or more. The skill listing as a whole is budgeted at 1% of the context window, and the least-used descriptions are shortened first. | <https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/measure.md><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills.md#skill-descriptions-are-cut-short><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* User-only skills (`disable-model-invocation: true`) add no description. Whether a `paths:`-scoped skill adds one before activation is unresolved (see Unverified). |
| `claude plugin list --json` entry shape | An array of objects with `id` (`<name>@inline` for a plugin loaded with `--plugin-dir`), `version`, `scope` (`session`), `enabled` and `installPath`, plus `errors` (strings) and `errorDetails` (`type`, `plugin`, `component`, `path`) when loading failed. The command exits 0 either way. | local reproduction (claude 2.1.280) with a throwaway `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` and a plugin whose `outputStyles` path is missing | 2026-09-26 | *Single pass.* CI fails when the expected id is absent, any `errors` entry exists or `enabled` is false. |

| JsonResource::withoutWrapping() semantics | Only affects the outermost resource response and does not remove `data` keys you add yourself; paginated resource collections are ALWAYS wrapped in `data` with `links`/`meta`, even after withoutWrapping(); Laravel won't double-wrap when toArray() already contains a `data` key, UNLESS the resource class sets the new public static bool $forceWrapping = true (Laravel 13 JsonResource property checked in ResourceResponse::haveDefaultWrapperAndDataIsUnwrapped). | <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/laravel/docs/13.x/eloquent-resources.md> (#data-wrapping)<br>laravel/framework v13.33.0 src/Illuminate/Http/Resources/Json/JsonResource.php:59, ResourceResponse.php:81-88 | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* |
| Custom envelope and data.data nesting (empirical) | Laravel 13.33, default wrapping: response()-&gt;json(['flag'=&gt;true,'data'=&gt;new PostResource($m),'message'=&gt;..]) gives flat data (JsonResource::jsonSerialize() = resolve() with no wrap). The same with PostResource::collection($paginator) gives items only, and links/meta are SILENTLY DROPPED. data.data happens only when embedding -&gt;response()-&gt;getData(true) (both single and paginated). (new PostResource($m))-&gt;additional(['flag'=&gt;true,'message'=&gt;'ok']) gives flat {data, flag, message} and paginated additional() gives {data, links, meta, flag, message}; with withoutWrapping()+additional() the payload is still wrapped in data. | sandbox Pest feature test against Laravel 13.33.0, 2026-09-25 | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked.* |

Scope: ESLint 9/10 and flat config; eslint-config-next; typescript-eslint and TypeScript 7; sonarjs; eslint-comments; knip; jscpd; dependency-cruiser; Prettier; Vitest; Playwright and axe; jsx-a11y; markdownlint; Node release lines. 79 entries.

| expo-localization RTL native behaviour SDK ≤57 | iOS (v57): on module OnCreate, if forcesRTL is set it calls allowRTL(true) and forceRTL(forcesRTL). Otherwise, if supportsRTL is set, it calls allowRTL(supportsRTL) and forceRTL(supportsRTL ? isRTLPreferredForCurrentLocale() : false). So supportsRTL:true re-derives direction from the DEVICE language on every module creation. Android (v57): supportsRTL → allowRTL; forceRTL(false) only when forcesRTL=="false"; forceRTL(true) when forcesRTL=="true". | npm pack expo-localization@57.0.2 ios/LocalizationModule.swift, android/.../LocalizationModule.kt | 2026-09-25 | *Re-checked, refined.* Re-check: Precise iOS behaviour: forcesRTL:false (key present) runs allowRTL(true) and forceRTL(isRTLPreferredForCurrentLocale()), not forceRTL(false). With neither key set, iOS makes no RTL calls. Android's forceRTL(false) branch only runs when supportsRTL is also set. |

| expo-secure-store API and limits | expo-secure-store@57.0.4. Async: setItemAsync(key,value,opts), getItemAsync, deleteItemAsync, isAvailableAsync. Synchronous: setItem(key,value,opts) and getItem(key,opts), which block the JS thread. There is no synchronous delete. canUseBiometricAuthentication() is also available. Keys may contain alphanumerics, '.', '-' and '\_'. Platforms: Android, iOS, tvOS, not web. Expo enforces no size limit, but 'historically some iOS releases refused values above roughly 2048 bytes'. The iOS keychain can survive a reinstall; Android data is removed on uninstall. Options: keychainAccessible (default WHEN\_UNLOCKED), requireAuthentication, keychainService, accessGroup. | lefthook glob semantics | The default matcher is gobwas and is case-insensitive. A pattern like '\*.rb' matches nested paths (a unit test expects 'folder/subfolder/0.rb'). '\*\*' matches 1 or more directories, not zero. 'glob\_matcher: doublestar' (added 2.0.4) switches to bash-like behaviour. glob can be a list (&gt;=1.10.10). Globs are computed from the repo root even when 'root' is set | <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evilmartians/lefthook/master/docs/configuration/glob.md><br><https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evilmartians/lefthook/master/docs/configuration/glob_matcher.md><br>lefthook v2.1.14 internal/run/controller/filter/filter.go + filter\_test.go (git clone --branch v2.1.14) | 2026-09-25 | *Single pass.* A glob like `*.php` matches nested PHP files. Avoid `**/*.php` under the default matcher: it misses root-level files. |
| lefthook jobs vs commands | 'jobs' (list, supports groups with parallel/piped) added in 1.10.0 and is used in current docs; 'commands' (named map) is still supported | <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evilmartians/lefthook/master/docs/configuration/jobs.md><br><https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evilmartians/lefthook/master/docs/configuration/Commands.md> | 2026-09-25 | *Single pass.* |

| `cache-mode` workflow key | Top-level and job-level `cache-mode` (read, write, write-only, none) exists in workflow syntax; actionlint 1.7.12 (and its main branch) rejects it as an unexpected key. | <https://docs.github.com/en/actions/reference/workflows-and-actions/workflow-syntax#cache-mode><br><https://github.com/rhysd/actionlint/blob/v1.7.12/parse.go> | 2026-09-25 | *Found on re-check.* |
| Ruleset rules for tags: creation, update, deletion | `{"type":"creation"}` and `{"type":"deletion"}` take no parameters; `{"type":"update"}` takes optional `parameters.update_allows_fetch_and_merge` (boolean). Each restricts the action on matching refs to actors with bypass permission. Ruleset `target` is `branch`, `tag` or `push`. `ref_name.include` documents `~DEFAULT_BRANCH` and `~ALL` ("all branches"), so a tag ruleset names patterns such as `refs/tags/*`. | GitHub REST OpenAPI schemas repository-rule-creation, repository-rule-update, repository-rule-deletion and repository-ruleset-conditions (github/rest-api-description main at d66b5ecab2cd77c824ec336433a262241c2abc96) | 2026-09-26 | *Single pass.* `bypass_mode: pull_request` applies to branch rulesets only, so the admin bypass of a tag ruleset uses `always`. |
| Actions permission endpoints | `PUT /repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/permissions`: body `enabled` (required), `allowed_actions` (all\|local\_only\|selected), `sha_pinning_required`. `PUT /repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/permissions/workflow`: body `default_workflow_permissions` (read\|write), `can_approve_pull_request_reviews`. | GitHub REST OpenAPI operations actions/set-github-actions-permissions-repository and actions/set-github-actions-default-workflow-permissions-repository (same commit) | 2026-09-26 | *Single pass.* |
| gh 2.101.0 flags used by the workflows | `gh release create <tag> --title --notes-file --verify-tag --prerelease` (`--verify-tag` aborts when the tag does not exist on the remote); `gh run download <run-id> --name <artifact> --dir <dir>` (it does not check the run's status, so a later job of the same run can download an artifact); `gh issue list --state --search` with `--json`/`--jq`; `gh issue create --title --body-file`; `gh issue edit <number> --body-file`; `gh secret set <name>` reads the value from standard input when `--body` is absent. `GH_REPO` selects the repository. | <https://github.com/cli/cli/tree/v2.101.0/pkg/cmd> (release/create, run/download, issue/list, issue/create, issue/edit, secret/set) | 2026-09-26 | *Single pass.* gh 2.101.0 is preinstalled on the ubuntu-24.04 image. |
| `node --test <directory>` on Node 22 | `node --test test/hooks` treats the argument as a module path and fails (`Cannot find module …/test/hooks`). A quoted glob such as `node --test "test/hooks/**/*.test.mjs"` runs every matching file and behaves the same on every OS. | local reproduction (Node 22.23.1) | 2026-09-26 | *Single pass.* |
| actionlint 1.7.12 CLI | `-color` and `-no-color` are booleans (`-color=never` is a usage error). The shellcheck and pyflakes integrations use the binaries on `PATH` and are disabled, with a note under `-verbose`, when missing. | actionlint 1.7.12 `-help` and `-verbose` (local) | 2026-09-26 | *Single pass.* Put the pinned shellcheck on `PATH` before running actionlint so run blocks are checked with 0.11.0, not the runner's 0.9.0. |

## GitHub Action pins

Resolved with `git ls-remote` (annotated tags peeled to their commit) and re-resolved on 2026-09-25. Pin as `uses: owner/repo@<full SHA> # <tag>`. Every action here runs on node24 or is composite: GitHub removed Node 20 from hosted runners on 2026-09-23, so node20 majors such as `actions/checkout@v4` and `actions/setup-node@v4` are no longer usable.

| Action | Tag | Full SHA | Source | Notes | Verified on |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| actions/checkout | v7.0.1 | `3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1` | git ls-remote --tags <https://github.com/actions/checkout.git><br><https://github.com/actions/checkout/releases/tag/v7.0.1> | node24; lightweight tag. `persist-credentials` still defaults to true, so set `persist-credentials: false` in jobs that do not push. v7 refuses fork-PR checkout under pull\_request\_target/workflow\_run unless `allow-unsafe-pr-checkout` is set. | 2026-09-25 |
| actions/setup-node | v7.0.0 | `820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020` | git ls-remote --tags <https://github.com/actions/setup-node.git><br><https://github.com/actions/setup-node/releases/tag/v7.0.0> | node24; ESM; `package-manager-cache` defaults to true. | 2026-09-25 |
| shivammathur/setup-php | 2.37.2 | `f3e473d116dcccaddc5834248c87452386958240` | git ls-remote --tags <https://github.com/shivammathur/setup-php.git> | node24. The tag has no `v`; comment it as `# 2.37.2`. The floating `v2` tag is an annotated tag object (b604ade2a87db23f8871b7182e69ec5e75effb45) that peels to the same commit. Use `coverage: pcov` and include `intl`. | 2026-09-25 |

## Unverified — re-check before use

Do not rely on these values without re-checking them. The first table lists items the independent re-check marked uncertain, or on which two re-check passes disagreed. The second lists upstream events that will change verified values soon. The third lists behaviour that no pass verified.

### Marked uncertain on re-check

| Tool / item | Last known value | Source | Checked on | Why unverified / what to do |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Single-file typed ESLint run (PostToolUse time box) | 3.0–4.5 s per file on one Apple Silicon machine (typed 3.8–4.5 s; cache hit 3.0 s; untyped 3.0 s). The first pass measured 2.7–2.9 s. | local measurement (Next + strictTypeChecked + sonarjs + eslint-comments) | 2026-09-25 | Machine- and project-dependent; most time is process and config load. Measure on target hardware before fixing the hook budget. |
| release-please on a dev→main promotion flow | History is full ancestry in `git log` date order, but the walk stops at the last release SHA, so a dev commit dated before the previous release merge and promoted later can be dropped from the next changelog and bump. One workflow using `target-branch: ${{ github.ref_name }}` can serve several branches. | <https://github.com/googleapis/release-please-action/blob/45996ed1f6d02564a971a2fa1b5860e934307cf7/README.md><br><https://github.com/googleapis/release-please/blob/v17.6.0/src/manifest.ts><br><https://docs.github.com/public/fpt/schema.docs.graphql> | 2026-09-25 | From reading the source, not a documented guarantee. Prototype with `release-please --dry-run` in a sandbox repo before relying on it. |
| `can_approve_pull_request_reviews` enables PR creation by Actions | Defaults confirmed: GITHUB\_TOKEN read-only for contents/packages; Actions may not create or approve PRs on new personal repos. The REST field is documented only as allowing Actions to "submit approving pull request reviews". | <https://docs.github.com/en/repositories/managing-your-repositorys-settings-and-features/enabling-features-for-your-repository/managing-github-actions-settings-for-a-repository><br>GitHub REST OpenAPI actions/set-github-actions-default-workflow-permissions-repository | 2026-09-25 | Likely maps to the single UI checkbox "Allow GitHub Actions to create and approve pull requests". Verify with a GET after the PUT and a test run, or set it in the UI. |
| Always-on context of a `paths:`-scoped plugin skill before activation | The two re-check passes disagree. One ran `claude -p "/context" --plugin-dir <dir>` (claude 2.1.280) with no matching files present and saw the `paths:` skill in the Skills table at about 340 tokens, like an unscoped skill. The other, citing open issue #49835, treats a `paths:` skill as hidden (absent from the `/` menu and not invocable by name) until a matching file is read or edited, and so as absent from the listing. `claude plugin details` counts it as always-on either way. | local reproduction (claude 2.1.280)<br><https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/49835><br><https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills.md#frontmatter-reference> | 2026-09-25 | The docs don't say. Measure with `/context` in an authenticated session before relying on `paths:` to reduce context; until then, budget such skills as always-on. |

### Not verified by any pass

| Item | What is known | How to verify |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `gh auth refresh -s workflow` | Only `gh auth login --scopes` and the automatic `workflow` scope for the gh credential helper were verified. | Check `gh auth refresh --help` once gh is installed. |
| Re-adding a marketplace that already exists under the same name | The docs say a `marketplace add` whose source or ref differs from a name declared in any settings file is refused (`its network source differs`), and `marketplace add` writes its own entry to user settings. So `claude plugin marketplace add pbakaus/impeccable#skill-v4.3.1` after an unpinned add of `impeccable` is expected to be refused. Not executed. | Test in a throwaway `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`. The documented fixes are editing the `ref` in settings, or removing the marketplace (which uninstalls its plugins) and adding it again. |
| Project `.mcp.json` server vs an installed plugin server | Docs say plugins dedupe "by endpoint"; the chrome-devtools-mcp args differ, so both may run. | Test with the plugin installed. |
| `--allowed-url-pattern` strings for local dev origins | URLPattern syntax for localhost with any port on Chrome 155. | Test before adding to the template. |
| Windows behaviour | Exec-form hooks with `node`, and `"command": "npx"` for stdio MCP servers without a `cmd /c` wrapper, are documented but untested on Windows. | Windows CI job or manual check. |
| openapi-typescript 7.13.0 with TypeScript 6/7 | Peer `typescript ^5.x`; npm 10 may warn or fail with ERESOLVE on TS 6.x. | Scratch install in the plan phase. |
| Scramble extension API for problem+json responses | Default export documents 422 as Laravel's `{message, errors}` with application/json. | Read the Scramble extension docs before promising the contract matches RFC 9457. |
| Pint agent-mode opt-out | Pint 1.32.1 prints JSON under an agent regardless of `PAO_DISABLE` and `--format=txt`; no opt-out variable found. | Check the laravel/pint source; rely on the exit code meanwhile. |
| PHPMD 2.15.0 on PHP 8.4/8.5 | Last stable release 2023-12-11; not tested on current PHP. | Prefer tomasvotruba/cognitive-complexity (verified). |
| Pest `--list-tests` | Not verified as a way to prove a gate test is discovered. | Use `pest --filter '<RULE-ID>'` and assert at least one test ran. |
| Hermes Intl.PluralRules on React Native 0.86 | Hermes docs and i18next docs say it is missing; the Hermes doc may lag Hermes V1. | Runtime check of `typeof Intl.PluralRules` on device. |
| expo-localization RTL reset on JS reload | Whether the OnCreate RTL reset runs on every JS reload or only on cold start (SDK ≤57 iOS with supportsRTL; SDK 58 default). | On-device test on both platforms before labelling the RTL contract FORCED. |
| GitHub Actions `integration_id` 15368 for required checks | Community-sourced only. | Leave `integration_id` unset. |
| Secret-scanning push protection REST call | Property names confirmed from the REST reference; the PATCH was not executed. | Execute and read back during repository bootstrap. |
| Actions that still declare node20 | Whether they fail or are force-run on Node 24 after 2026-09-23 is not stated. | Pin node24 releases regardless. |
| Composer `version` field and Packagist for monorepo PHP packages | Packagist reads composer.json from a repository root; a subdirectory package probably needs a split repo. | Check before publishing the PHP gate package. |
| Betterleaks release checksums | Checksum assets were not checked. | Check before any migration from gitleaks. |
| `claude plugin validate` exit code 2 | Documented for a validator failure such as an unreadable path. On 2.1.280 a missing path, an unreadable file or directory and invalid JSON all exit 1. | Treat any non-zero exit as failure; don't branch on 2. |
| `autoUpdate` in a committed project `.claude/settings.json` | The settings reference allows `autoUpdate` on any `extraKnownMarketplaces` entry, and the loading page ranks a settings-file value first; the hosting page mentions only users and admins. | Test in a trusted scratch repository. |
| Two projects pinning different refs of one marketplace name | There is one `known_marketplaces.json` per user. Whether switching between projects that declare different refs re-clones the marketplace, or lets one project's pin leak into the other, is undocumented. | Test with two scratch repositories and a throwaway `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`. |
| `claude plugin marketplace add owner/repo#<40-character SHA>` | The docs describe `ref` as a branch or tag only. | Test in a throwaway `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`. |
| Minimum Claude Code version for the plugin | Exec-form hook `args` needs 2.1.139+, one-step `/plugin install --marketplace` 2.1.275+, `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS` 2.1.280+. What an older client does with a hook that has `args` (for example, ignoring them and running bare `node`) is undocumented. | Test the lowest version to be documented before stating it. |
| `claude plugin eval` on cases outside the plugin directory | Documented: a single `prompt.md` whose `plugins:` field points at the plugin relative to the case directory. Not run, because it needs paid model credentials. | One authenticated run. |
| Uninstall and saved `userConfig` values | Whether uninstalling from the last scope removes `pluginConfigs` values and stored secrets is not documented beyond the 2.1.282 fixes. | Test in a throwaway `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`. |

## Maintaining this file

- Every row carries its own **Verified on** (or **Checked on**) date. When you re-verify a row, update its value, source and date together. The only exceptions are the [Key pins at a glance](#key-pins-at-a-glance) summary, whose rows repeat values that are dated in the domain tables, and the rows under "Not verified by any pass", which by definition have no verification date yet.
- The watch workflow opens an issue listing rows older than 90 days and every data row that has no date (the "Not verified by any pass" rows included, since each still needs a verification). Treat security floors (Next.js, runner Node versions) and pinned SHAs as due sooner when an upstream security release is announced.
- Resolve Action SHAs with `git ls-remote --tags https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>.git 'refs/tags/<tag>' 'refs/tags/<tag>^{}'` and pin the peeled commit (the `^{}` line) when the tag is annotated.
- Never copy a version from memory or from another project. If a value cannot be confirmed, move the row to [Unverified — re-check before use](#unverified--re-check-before-use).
